HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B002","root_000913/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1e120b0154f68c172358","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:2:5\",\"root_000913\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Boşa çıkma, planın sonunda bulunamaz ve geri kazanılamaz bir kalıntı gibi etkin olmaktan çıkmasını da içerir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B001","root_000603/B001","root_001334/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_260b98850ba027faeeca","text":"4. **A non-symmetric answer to scheming.** Lexical evidence: ك ي د B002, stratagem; ر س ل B001 in 105:3, sending forth/release; ء ب ل B003, successive or scattered groups; ر م ي B001, throwing/casting. Structural cue: their كيد is answered not by another explicit كيد-root term, but by release, grouping, and projectile action. Assigned role: كيد defines their mode as contrivance; إرسال/أبابيل/رمي define the counter-mode as ordered dispatch. Abductive move: I supply that divine action avoids mirroring their strategy and instead changes the operating field. Reading change: the ayah reads as asymmetrical defeat: a scheme is not out-plotted so much as displaced into a system where it cannot aim.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B001","root_000603/B006","root_000913/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3483087e05b0c9b0f352","text":"3. **`تَضْلِيل` becomes a reversal of aim.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `تَضْلِيلٍ` carries `ض ل ل` B001, “going astray from guidance and aim”; contextual `تَرْمِيهِم` carries `ر م ي` B001, physical projectile casting, and B006, progressing until a limit is reached. **Structural cue:** their operation is made to lose its aim, whereas the later projectiles are grammatically directed at `هُمْ` and are followed by a resulting transformation. **Assigned role:** B001 removes effective trajectory from the plot; the `ر م ي` branches give the counter-action a target and endpoint. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies direction toward them and the final result; I infer successful arrival and contrast it with their lost aim. **Reading change:** `تَضْلِيل` acquires an operational and almost ballistic precision: one trajectory is derailed while the counter-trajectory reaches its mark.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002","root_000913/B001","root_000913/B003","root_001334/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_35a0a4f8a3dfa88047e9","text":"1. **Plot turned into a state of misdirection.** Lexical evidence: ج ع ل B002, turning something into a state; ك ي د B002, plotting and stratagem; ض ل ل B001, going astray from guidance and aim; ض ل ل B003, losing or failing to locate something. Structural cue: the construction \"يجعل كيدهم في تضليل\" places the plot inside a field of loss, and 105:5 repeats جعل with the people themselves as object. Assigned role: كيد supplies intentional planning; جعل supplies state conversion; ضلل supplies the new medium in which the plan operates. Abductive move: I infer that the plot is not merely blocked from outside but made to continue inside a wrong space; the packet supplies the containment phrase \"في\" and the branches of turning/loss. Reading change: the ayah reads as misdirection of agency itself, not only failure of a scheme.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B002","root_000913/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3ec50219fd4483c85b5f","text":"4. **Misguiding also becomes disappearance into residue.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `تَضْلِيلٍ` activates `ض ل ل` B002, “being hidden or disappearing,” and B003, “losing or failing to locate something”; later `مَّأْكُولٍ` activates `ء ك ل` B006, “being eaten away, decay,” beside `ع ص ف` B001, broken crop residue. **Structural cue:** the abstract loss of the plot precedes a simile of matter whose usable substance has been consumed and fragmented. **Assigned role:** B002-B003 remove the plot from effective presence; the final erosion image gives that removal a material analogue. **Abductive move:** the packet does not state that the plot physically decays; I infer that the final state concretizes the earlier abstract disappearance. **Reading change:** `فِى تَضْلِيلٍ` means more than wrong direction: the scheme is made to vanish as an effective, recoverable thing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002","root_000913/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_40c4863045df01975f8d","text":"105:4 made the “aim” component of `ض ل ل` B001 newly concrete: the plot loses direction just before a cast explicitly directed at `هُمْ` reaches a result. 105:5 then supplied two retrospective activations at once—the repeated `ج ع ل` B002 revealed a plan-to-planners transformation, and eaten chaff activated `ض ل ل` B002-B003 as disappearance or unrecoverable loss rather than error alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B001","root_001334/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4a9142f37b52e77abc5b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:2:3\",\"root_001334\",[\"B002\"]],[\"105:2:5\",\"root_000913\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Karşılık, onların planını başka bir planla taklit etmek yerine çalışma alanını değiştirerek hedef alma yetisini ortadan kaldırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001334/B001","root_001334/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5c97860e2454bdb8a08e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:2:3\",\"root_001334\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Plan için harcanan çaba, kendi sahiplerini tüketen sonuca yakıt olmuş gibi okunur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6b058f4856d0fc6db165","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:2:5\",\"root_000913\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Onların yönelimi hedefini yitirirken karşı eylemin onlara ulaşıp sonuç vermesi, boşa çıkmayı yönlerin tersine çevrilmesi olarak keskinleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B001","root_000844/B001","root_000913/B002","root_000913/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_728507a7ba1790f7a4bf","text":"3. **Disappearance as the internal logic of \"تضليل\".** Lexical evidence: ض ل ل B002, being hidden or disappearing; ض ل ل B005, stray animal in a wasteland; ص ح ب B001 and ف ي ل B004 from 105:1, a company tied to an animal; ر م ي B001/ح ج ر B003 in 105:4, stones cast against them. Structural cue: the known, named company of 105:1 loses its way before becoming unrecognizable chaff in 105:5. Assigned role: ضلل B002/B005 turns the military company into something lost from its owner/path and then into vanishing material. Abductive move: I infer a progression from strategic deviation to social/material disappearance; the packet supplies \"companions,\" \"elephant,\" \"misdirection,\" and final debris. Reading change: \"تضليل\" includes erasure and stray-ness, not only intellectual error.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7f855fe920fd501a1e70","text":"1. **The first `جعل` begins a two-stage transformation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `يَجْعَلْ` carries `ج ع ل` B002, “turning something into a state”; 105:5 repeats the same root and branch in `فَجَعَلَهُمْ`. **Structural cue:** the first construction takes `كَيْدَهُمْ` into the state-space `فِى تَضْلِيلٍ`, while the final construction takes `هُمْ` into the comparison `كَعَصْفٍ مَّأْكُولٍ`. **Assigned role:** the first B002 disables the collective’s operative design; the second makes the collective itself bear a material result. **Abductive move:** sequence is supplied, but I infer the directional arrow that strategic neutralization precedes and enables bodily/material reduction. **Reading change:** this ayah becomes the first half of a deliberate conversion pipeline: plan first, planners last.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001334/B001","root_001334/B002","root_001334/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9c26b3c94623509de6a8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:2:3\",\"root_001334\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Plan, gizli tasarıdan daha genişler ve yoğun emek ile savaş gücü taşıyan uygulanmış bir harekâtı da kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9d786aa400558dd4ce73","text":"105:5 made the two جعل clauses behave like brackets: first the كيد is turned into تضليل, then the plotters themselves are turned into عصف مأكول. Structural cue: the effort named كيد precedes the final passive image \"مأكول\"; the exertion of 105:2 ends as consumed residue in 105:5. Assigned role: كيد B001/B004 makes their plan a strenuous martial undertaking; the final عصف/أكل complex gives that undertaking a material endpoint. Abductive move: I infer that the labor of the plot feeds the process that undoes it; the packet supplies effort/war and eaten debris, while I supply the self-consuming arrow.
